<script>
(function() {
  function initKsMenu() {

    var items = document.querySelectorAll('#desktopNav .nav-item');
    items.forEach(function(it) {
      if (!it.querySelector('.mega')) return;
      it.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
        items.forEach(function(i) { i.classList.remove('open'); });
        it.classList.add('open');
      });
      it.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
        it.classList.remove('open');
      });
    });

    var searchModal = document.getElementById('searchModal');
    var searchModalClose = document.getElementById('searchModalClose');
    var searchInputField = document.getElementById('searchInput');
    var searchBtn = document.getElementById('searchBtn');

    function performSearch(query) {
      if (!query.trim()) return;
      var searchUrl = '{{ Urls.BaseUrl }}busca?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query.trim());
      window.location.href = searchUrl;
    }

    function openSearchModal() {
      if (!searchModal) return;
      searchModal.classList.add('open');
      setTimeout(function() { if (searchInputField) searchInputField.focus(); }, 100);
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }

    function closeSearchModal() {
      if (!searchModal) return;
      searchModal.classList.remove('open');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }

    if (searchBtn) {
      searchBtn.addEventListener('click', openSearchModal);
    }

    if (searchModalClose) {
      searchModalClose.addEventListener('click', closeSearchModal);
    }

    if (searchModal) {
      searchModal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        if (e.target === searchModal) closeSearchModal();
      });
    }

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && searchModal && searchModal.classList.contains('open')) {
        closeSearchModal();
      }
    });

    if (searchInputField) {
      searchInputField.addEventListener('keypress', function(e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') performSearch(searchInputField.value);
      });
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('.search-suggestions span').forEach(function(sug) {
      sug.addEventListener('click', function() {
        performSearch(sug.innerText);
      });
    });

    var drawer = document.getElementById('mobileDrawer');
    var backdrop = document.getElementById('mobileBackdrop');

    function openDrawer() {
      if (drawer) drawer.classList.add('open');
      if (backdrop) backdrop.classList.add('open');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    }

    function closeDrawer() {
      if (drawer) drawer.classList.remove('open');
      if (backdrop) backdrop.classList.remove('open');
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }

    var mobileToggle = document.getElementById('mobileToggle');
    var mobileClose = document.getElementById('mobileClose');

    if (mobileToggle) mobileToggle.addEventListener('click', openDrawer);
    if (mobileClose) mobileClose.addEventListener('click', closeDrawer);
    if (backdrop) backdrop.addEventListener('click', closeDrawer);

    document.querySelectorAll('.m-item .m-link').forEach(function(link) {
      link.addEventListener('click', function() {
        var item = link.parentElement;
        if (!item.querySelector('.m-sub')) return;
        item.classList.toggle('open');
      });
    });

    var mobileSearchInput = document.getElementById('mobileSearchInput');
    if (mobileSearchInput) {
      mobileSearchInput.addEventListener('focus', function() {
        closeDrawer();
        openSearchModal();
      });
    }

    var logoImg = document.querySelector('.logo img');
    if (logoImg) {
      if (logoImg.complete && logoImg.naturalWidth === 0) {
        logoImg.style.display = 'none';
        var fallback = logoImg.nextElementSibling;
        if (fallback) fallback.style.display = 'flex';
      }
      logoImg.addEventListener('error', function() {
        logoImg.style.display = 'none';
        var fb = logoImg.nextElementSibling;
        if (fb) fb.style.display = 'flex';
      });
    }

  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initKsMenu);
  } else {
    initKsMenu();
  }
})();
</script>

<style>

function initBannerNav(){
  var sl=document.querySelector('.full-banner.newBanners .wd-marketing-banner.slider.slick-initialized');
  if(!sl) return false;
  var $sl=$(sl);
  var _list=sl.querySelector('.slick-list');var _track=sl.querySelector('.slick-track');
  function syncBanner(idx){
    var realSlides=sl.querySelectorAll('.slick-slide:not(.slick-cloned)');
    var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
    if(idx<0||idx>=ctrlLis.length)return;
    realSlides.forEach(function(s,i){s.classList.toggle('banner-show',i===idx&&!s.classList.contains('slider-controller'));});
    ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){var a=(i===idx);li.className=a?'current':'';li.style.setProperty('background',a?'#F3C969':'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)','important');li.style.setProperty('width',a?'14px':'10px','important');li.style.setProperty('height',a?'14px':'10px','important');});
  }
  $sl.off('afterChange.ksbnav').on('afterChange.ksbnav',function(e,slick,cur){setTimeout(function(){syncBanner(cur);},50);});
  var prevBtn=sl.querySelector('.slick-prev');
  var nextBtn=sl.querySelector('.slick-next');
  if(prevBtn){prevBtn.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');prevBtn.style.setProperty('z-index','20','important');prevBtn.addEventListener('click',function(){$sl.slick('slickPrev');});}
  if(nextBtn){nextBtn.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');nextBtn.style.setProperty('z-index','20','important');nextBtn.addEventListener('click',function(){$sl.slick('slickNext');});}
  var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
  ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){
    li.style.cursor='pointer';
    li.onclick=function(){$sl.slick('slickGoTo',i);};
  });
  syncBanner(0);
  return true;
}
var _bnTries=0;
function tryBannerNav(){if(!initBannerNav()&&_bnTries++<30) setTimeout(tryBannerNav,300);}

(function(){
var _answers = {};

function scoreProduct(p, a){
  var score = 0;
  if(p.category.indexOf(a.needType||'mattress')>=0) score+=100;
  if(p.sizes&&p.sizes[a.size||'casal']) score+=50;
  if(p.comfort.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=30;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.painPoint||'comfort')>=0) score+=40;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=20;
  if(p.bestFor.indexOf(a.sleepPos||'side')>=0) score+=25;
  if((a.painPoint==='delivery')&&p.format==='box_mattress') score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='partner_motion')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='heat'||a.comfort==='cooling')&&p.coolingTechnology) score+=50;
  if((a.sleepPos==='couple'||a.sleepPos==='moves')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=30;
  return score;
}
function formatMoney(v){ return 'R$ '+v; }
function offPct(de,por){
  var d=parseFloat(de.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  var p=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(d)||isNaN(p)||d<=0) return '';
  return Math.round((1-p/d)*100)+'% OFF';
}
function nightCost(por,years){
  if(!years||years<0.5) return '';
  var num=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(num)) return '';
  var nights=years*365;
  var n=(num/nights).toFixed(2).replace('.',',');
  return '&#127769; R$ '+n+'/noite por '+years+(years===1?' ano':' anos');
}
function chips(p){
  var arr=[];
  if(p.trialNights>0) arr.push('&#127769; '+p.trialNights+' noites de teste');
  if(p.guaranteeYears>=1) arr.push('&#128737; '+p.guaranteeYears+(p.guaranteeYears<2?' ano':' anos')+' de garantia');
  else if(p.guaranteeYears>0) arr.push('&#128737; 3 meses de garantia');
  arr.push('&#128666; Frete gr&aacute;tis');
  if(p.format==='box_mattress') arr.push('&#128230; Cabe em elevador');
  return arr;
}

}function showStep(step){
  var all=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-q');
  for(var i=0;i<all.length;i++) all[i].classList.remove('active');
  if(step==='results'){
    showResults();
  } else {
    var el=document.getElementById('ksq-'+step);
    if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  }
}

  scored.sort(function(x,y){ return y.score-x.score; });
  var matching = scored.filter(function(x){ return x.p.category.indexOf(a.needType||'mattress')>=0; });
  if(!matching.length) matching=scored;
  
  var slots=[];
  if(matching[0]) slots.push({item:matching[0].p,badge:'best',label:'MAIS RECOMENDADO',score:matching[0].score});
  var notS1=matching.filter(function(x){return !slots.some(function(s){return s.item===x.p;});});
  var budget=notS1.filter(function(x){return x.p.tier==='entry'||x.p.tier==='mid';});
  var s2=budget[0]||notS1[0];

        profHtml+='<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.7);font-size:.8em;font-weight:600;margin:8px 0 6px">Evite:</div>';
        avoidItems.forEach(function(it){profHtml+='<div style="color:rgba(255,180,180,.85);font-size:.82em;margin-bottom:4px">&#10006; '+it+'</div>';});
      }
      profHtml+='</div>';
    }
    profHtml+='</div>';
    profBlock.innerHTML=profHtml;
    return;
  }
  var chips=profileChips(a);
  var benMap={
    stomach:'Mais suporte para quem dorme de bru\u00e7os',
    side:'Melhor distribui\u00e7\u00e3o de press\u00e3o para quem dorme de lado',

  if(a.sleepPos&&benMap[a.sleepPos]) bens.push(benMap[a.sleepPos]);
  if(a.painPoint&&benMap[a.painPoint]) bens.push(benMap[a.painPoint]);
  if(a.comfort&&benMap[a.comfort]) bens.push(benMap[a.comfort]);
  if(a.size&&benMap[a.size]) bens.push(benMap[a.size]);
  var html='<div style="margin-bottom:10px">';
  html+='<div style="color:#F3C969;font-weight:700;font-size:1em;letter-spacing:.01em;margin-bottom:4px">'+(isPillow?'Encontramos os travesseiros mais compatíveis para o seu perfil':'Encontramos os colchões mais compatíveis para o seu perfil')+'</div>';

  html+='</ul>';

  var discEl=document.getElementById('ksq-discarded');

      firm:'modelos muito macios, que n\u00e3o oferecem suporte suficiente para sua coluna',
      soft:'modelos firmes, que poderiam gerar press\u00e3o nos pontos de contato',
      medium:'modelos nos extremos de firmeza, focando no equil\u00edbrio',
      cooling:'modelos sem ventila\u00e7\u00e3o, que reteriam calor',
      back_pain:'modelos sem molas ensacadas, com menor capacidade de suporte postural',
      heat:'modelos com espuma tradicionais, que ret\u00eam mais calor',
      partner_motion:'modelos de espuma s\u00f3lida, que transferem mais movimento',
      sagging:'modelos de menor densidade, que afundariam mais rapidamente',
      stomach:'modelos muito macios, que causariam hiperextens\u00e3o na coluna lombar'
    };
    var reason=discMap[a.painPoint]||discMap[a.comfort]||discMap[a.sleepPos];
    if(reason){
      discEl.innerHTML='<div style="background:rgba(255,100,100,.05);border:1px solid rgba(255,100,100,.15);border-radius:10px;padding:14px 16px;margin:16px auto;max-width:1100px">'+'<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.45);font-size:.75em;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">Por que os outros foram descartados</div>'+'<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.65);font-size:.85em;line-height:1.5">N\u00e3o recomendamos '+reason+', com base no seu perfil informado.</div></div>';
    }
  }
})();
  var rEl=document.getElementById('ksq-results-area');
  if(rEl) rEl.classList.add('active');
}

    if(sleep==='back'){
      if(p.height==='medium') sc+=20;
    }
    p.score=sc;
  });
  catalog.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});
  return catalog;
}function handleClick(e){
  var opt=e.target.closest('.ksq-opt');
  if(!opt) return;
  var key=opt.getAttribute('data-key');
  var val=opt.getAttribute('data-val');
  var next=opt.getAttribute('data-next');
  if(key&&val) _answers[key]=val;
  var sibs=opt.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<sibs.length;i++) sibs[i].classList.remove('selected');
  opt.classList.add('selected');
  if(next==='results'){
    setTimeout(showResults,200);
  } else if(next){
    setTimeout(function(){showStep(next);},200);
  }
}

function handleRestart(){
  _answers={};
  var opts=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<opts.length;i++) opts[i].classList.remove('selected');
  var rEl=document.getElementById('ksq-results-area');
  if(rEl) rEl.classList.remove('active');
  showStep('q0');
  setTimeout(function(){var ksqW=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');if(ksqW){var top=ksqW.getBoundingClientRect().top+document.documentElement.scrollTop-20;document.documentElement.scrollTop=top;}},50);
}

function init(){
  var wrap=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');
  if(!wrap){setTimeout(init,300);return;}
  wrap.addEventListener('click',handleClick);
  var rb=document.getElementById('ksq-restart-btn');
  if(rb) rb.addEventListener('click',handleRestart);
  showStep('q0');
}

function tryInit(){if(document.getElementById('ksq-wrap')){init();}else{setTimeout(tryInit,100);}}
if(document.readyState==='loading'){
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,200);});
} else {
  setTimeout(tryInit,200);
}
window.addEventListener('load',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,500);});
})();
</script>

(function(){
function initBannerNav(){
  var sl=document.querySelector('.full-banner.newBanners .wd-marketing-banner.slider.slick-initialized');
  if(!sl) return false;
  var $sl=$(sl);
  function syncBanner(idx){
    var realSlides=sl.querySelectorAll('.slick-slide:not(.slick-cloned)');
    var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
    if(idx<0||idx>=ctrlLis.length)return;
    realSlides.forEach(function(s,i){s.classList.toggle('banner-show',i===idx&&!s.classList.contains('slider-controller'));});
    ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){var a=(i===idx);li.className=a?'current':'';li.style.setProperty('background',a?'#F3C969':'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)','important');li.style.setProperty('width',a?'14px':'10px','important');li.style.setProperty('height',a?'14px':'10px','important');});
  }
  $sl.off('afterChange.ksbnav').on('afterChange.ksbnav',function(e,slick,cur){setTimeout(function(){syncBanner(cur);},50);});
  var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
  ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){
    li.style.cursor='pointer';
    li.onclick=function(){$sl.slick('slickGoTo',i);};
  });
  syncBanner(0);
  return true;
}
var _bnTries=0;
function tryBannerNav(){if(!initBannerNav()&&_bnTries++<30) setTimeout(tryBannerNav,300);}
tryBannerNav();
})();

(function(){
var _answers = {};

function scoreProduct(p, a){
  var score = 0;
  if(p.category.indexOf(a.needType||'mattress')>=0) score+=100;
  if(p.sizes&&p.sizes[a.size||'casal']) score+=50;
  if(p.comfort.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=30;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.painPoint||'comfort')>=0) score+=40;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=20;
  if(p.bestFor.indexOf(a.sleepPos||'side')>=0) score+=25;
  if((a.painPoint==='delivery')&&p.format==='box_mattress') score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='partner_motion')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='heat'||a.comfort==='cooling')&&p.coolingTechnology) score+=50;
  if((a.sleepPos==='couple'||a.sleepPos==='moves')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=30;
  return score;
}
function formatMoney(v){ return 'R$ '+v; }
function offPct(de,por){
  var d=parseFloat(de.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  var p=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(d)||isNaN(p)||d<=0) return '';
  return Math.round((1-p/d)*100)+'% OFF';
}
function nightCost(por,years){
  if(!years||years<0.5) return '';
  var num=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(num)) return '';
  var nights=years*365;
  var n=(num/nights).toFixed(2).replace('.',',');
  return '&#127769; R$ '+n+'/noite por '+years+(years===1?' ano':' anos');
}
function chips(p){
  var arr=[];
  if(p.trialNights>0) arr.push('&#127769; '+p.trialNights+' noites de teste');
  if(p.guaranteeYears>=1) arr.push('&#128737; '+p.guaranteeYears+(p.guaranteeYears<2?' ano':' anos')+' de garantia');
  else if(p.guaranteeYears>0) arr.push('&#128737; 3 meses de garantia');
  arr.push('&#128666; Frete gr&aacute;tis');
  if(p.format==='box_mattress') arr.push('&#128230; Cabe em elevador');
  return arr;
}

function profileChips(){
  var a=_answers;
  var arr=[];
  var isPillow=!!(a.pSleep||a.pPain||a.pFeel);
  if(isPillow){
    var sm={side:'Dorme de lado',back:'Dorme de costas',stomach:'Dorme de barriga',mixed:'Muda de posição'};
    var pm={neck_pain:'Dor no pescoço',heat:'Sente calor',sinks:'Travesseiro afunda',softness:'Prefere maciez',support:'Precisa de suporte',posture:'Ronca ou postura',cuddling:'Dorme abraçado'};
    var fm={very_soft:'Muito macio',balanced:'Equilibrado',firm:'Mais firme',cooling:'Fresco e ventilado'};
    if(a.pSleep&&sm[a.pSleep]) arr.push(sm[a.pSleep]);
    if(a.pPain&&pm[a.pPain]) arr.push(pm[a.pPain]);
    if(a.pFeel&&fm[a.pFeel]) arr.push(fm[a.pFeel]);
  } else {
    var smap={side:'Dorme de lado',back:'Dorme de costas',stomach:'Dorme de bruços',couple:'Casal',moves:'Se mexe muito'};
    var cmap={soft:'Prefere macio',firm:'Prefere firme',medium:'Preferê intermediário',cooling:'Sofre de calor'};
    var pmap={back_pain:'Tem dores nas costas',heat:'Sofre de calor',partner_motion:'Parceiro se mexe',sagging:'Colchão afundou',delivery:'Precisa de entrega fácil',comfort:'Busca mais conforto'};
    if(a.sleepPos&&smap[a.sleepPos]) arr.push(smap[a.sleepPos]);
    if(a.comfort&&cmap[a.comfort]) arr.push(cmap[a.comfort]);
    if(a.painPoint&&pmap[a.painPoint]) arr.push(pmap[a.painPoint]);
    if(a.size) arr.push('Tamanho: '+a.size.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+a.size.slice(1));
  }
  return arr;
}function showStep(step){
  var all=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-q');
  for(var i=0;i<all.length;i++) all[i].classList.remove('active');
  if(step==='results'){
    showResults();
  } else {
    var el=document.getElementById('ksq-'+step);
    if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  }
}
function showResults(){

  var matching = scored.filter(function(x){ return x.p.category.indexOf(a.needType||'mattress')>=0; });
  if(!matching.length) matching=scored;
  
  var slots=[];
  if(matching[0]) slots.push({item:matching[0].p,badge:'best',label:'MAIS RECOMENDADO',score:matching[0].score});
  var notS1=matching.filter(function(x){return !slots.some(function(s){return s.item===x.p;});});
  var budget=notS1.filter(function(x){return x.p.tier==='entry'||x.p.tier==='mid';});
  var s2=budget[0]||notS1[0];

  var notSel=matching.filter(function(x){return !slots.some(function(s){return s.item===x.p;});});

     bestFor:['back','mixed','stomach'],cooling:false,supportLevel:'low',softFeel:true,
     strengths:['softness','fluffy','comfort'],therapeutic:false,
     premiumLevel:'essencial',
     hero:'Maciez e conforto com excelente custo-beneficio.',
     url:'/travesseiro-toque-eleva',
     price:7900,priceFrom:15800,
     benefits:['Ultra macio','Confortavel','Acessivel','Facil lavar']}
  ];
  catalog.forEach(function(p){
    var sc=0;
    if(sleep&&p.bestFor.indexOf(sleep)>=0) sc+=40;
    if(pain==='neck_pain'&&p.strengths.indexOf('cervical')>=0) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='neck_pain'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=25;
    if(pain==='heat'&&p.cooling) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='heat'&&p.strengths.indexOf('cooling')>=0) sc+=15;
    if(pain==='sinks'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=40;
    if(pain==='sinks'&&p.supportLevel==='medium') sc+=20;
    if(pain==='softness'&&p.softFeel) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='support'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=50;
    if(pain==='posture'&&p.therapeutic) sc+=40;
    if(pain==='posture'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=20;
    if(pain==='cuddling'&&p.softFeel) sc+=30;
    if(pain==='cuddling'&&(p.id==='double_latex'||p.id==='pluma_sense')) sc+=20;
    if(feel==='very_soft'&&p.softFeel) sc+=30;
    if(feel==='balanced'&&p.supportLevel==='medium') sc+=30;
    if(feel==='firm'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=30;
    if(feel==='cooling'&&p.cooling) sc+=40;
    if(pref==='tech'&&(p.id==='memory_foam'||p.id==='purple_pillow')) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='fluffy'&&p.softFeel) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='natural'&&(p.id==='latex_flakes'||p.id==='double_latex')) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='value'&&p.premiumLevel==='essencial') sc+=25;
    if(budget==='essential'&&p.premiumLevel==='essencial') sc+=20;
    if(budget==='premium'&&p.premiumLevel==='premium') sc+=20;
    if(budget==='max'&&p.premiumLevel==='premium'&&p.price>=22900) sc+=20;
    if(sleep==='stomach'){
      if(p.height==='low') sc+=30;
      if(p.softFeel) sc+=15;
    }
    if(sleep==='side'){
      if(p.height==='medium'||p.height==='high') sc+=15;
      if(p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=15;
    }
    if(sleep==='back'){
      if(p.height==='medium') sc+=20;
    }
    p.score=sc;
  });
  catalog.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});
  return catalog;
}function handleClick(e){
  var opt=e.target.closest('.ksq-opt');
  if(!opt) return;
  var key=opt.getAttribute('data-key');
  var val=opt.getAttribute('data-val');
  var next=opt.getAttribute('data-next');
  if(key&&val) _answers[key]=val;
  var sibs=opt.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<sibs.length;i++) sibs[i].classList.remove('selected');
  opt.classList.add('selected');
  if(next==='results'){
    setTimeout(showResults,200);
  } else if(next){
    setTimeout(function(){showStep(next);},200);
  }
}

function handleRestart(){
  _answers={};
  var opts=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<opts.length;i++) opts[i].classList.remove('selected');
  var rEl=document.getElementById('ksq-results-area');
  if(rEl) rEl.classList.remove('active');
  showStep('q0');
  setTimeout(function(){var ksqW=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');if(ksqW){var top=ksqW.getBoundingClientRect().top+document.documentElement.scrollTop-20;document.documentElement.scrollTop=top;}},50);
}

function init(){
  var wrap=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');
  if(!wrap){setTimeout(init,300);return;}
  wrap.addEventListener('click',handleClick);
  var rb=document.getElementById('ksq-restart-btn');
  if(rb) rb.addEventListener('click',handleRestart);
  showStep('q0');
}

function tryInit(){if(document.getElementById('ksq-wrap')){init();}else{setTimeout(tryInit,100);}}
if(document.readyState==='loading'){
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,200);});
} else {
  setTimeout(tryInit,200);
}
window.addEventListener('load',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,500);});
})();
</script>

<script>
// DESKTOP MEGA MENU
  const items = document.querySelectorAll('#desktopNav .nav-item');
  items.forEach(it => {
    if(!it.querySelector('.mega')) return;
    it.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
      items.forEach(i => i.classList.remove('open'));
      it.classList.add('open');
    });
    it.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => it.classList.remove('open'));
  });

  // MODAL DE BUSCA - Integração com Linx
  const searchModal = document.getElementById('searchModal');
  const searchModalClose = document.getElementById('searchModalClose');
  const searchInputField = document.getElementById('searchInput');
  const searchBtn = document.getElementById('searchBtn');
  
  function performSearch(query) {
    if (!query.trim()) return;
    // INTEGRAÇÃO COM LINX: 
    // Redireciona para a página de busca padrão da Linx Commerce
    // Exemplo: https://www.kingstarcolchoes.com.br/busca?q=colchão+casal
    var searchUrl = '{{ Urls.BaseUrl }}busca?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query.trim());
    window.location.href = searchUrl;
  }
  
  function openSearchModal() {
    searchModal.classList.add('open');
    setTimeout(() => searchInputField.focus(), 100);
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  }
  
  function closeSearchModal() {
    searchModal.classList.remove('open');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
  }
  
  if (searchBtn) {
    searchBtn.addEventListener('click', openSearchModal);
  }
  
  if (searchModalClose) {
    searchModalClose.addEventListener('click', closeSearchModal);
  }
  
  searchModal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    if (e.target === searchModal) closeSearchModal();
  });
  
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && searchModal.classList.contains('open')) {
      closeSearchModal();
    }
  });
  
  // Buscar ao pressionar Enter
  searchInputField.addEventListener('keypress', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      performSearch(searchInputField.value);
    }
  });
  
  // Sugestões clicáveis
  document.querySelectorAll('.search-suggestions span').forEach(sug => {
    sug.addEventListener('click', () => {
      performSearch(sug.innerText);
    });
  });

  // MOBILE DRAWER
  const drawer = document.getElementById('mobileDrawer');
  const backdrop = document.getElementById('mobileBackdrop');
  const open = () => { drawer.classList.add('open'); backdrop.classList.add('open'); document.body.style.overflow='hidden'; };
  const close = () => { drawer.classList.remove('open'); backdrop.classList.remove('open'); document.body.style.overflow=''; };
  document.getElementById('mobileToggle').addEventListener('click', open);
  document.getElementById('mobileClose').addEventListener('click', close);
  backdrop.addEventListener('click', close);

  // MOBILE ACCORDIONS
  document.querySelectorAll('.m-item .m-link').forEach(link => {
    link.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const item = link.parentElement;
      if(!item.querySelector('.m-sub')) return;
      item.classList.toggle('open');
    });
  });

  // MOBILE SEARCH
  const mobileSearchInput = document.getElementById('mobileSearchInput');
  if (mobileSearchInput) {
    mobileSearchInput.addEventListener('focus', () => {
      close();
      openSearchModal();
    });
  }

  // FALLBACK DO LOGO
  const logoImg = document.querySelector('.logo img');
  if (logoImg && logoImg.complete && logoImg.naturalWidth === 0) {
    logoImg.style.display = 'none';
    const fallback = logoImg.nextElementSibling;
    if (fallback) fallback.style.display = 'flex';
  }
  if (logoImg) {
    logoImg.addEventListener('error', function() {
      logoImg.style.display = 'none';
      var fallback = logoImg.nextElementSibling;
      if (fallback) fallback.style.display = 'flex';
    });
  }
  } // fim initMenu

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initMenu);
  } else {
    initMenu();
  }
})();
</script>

(function(){
function initBannerNav(){
  var sl=document.querySelector('.full-banner.newBanners .wd-marketing-banner.slider.slick-initialized');
  if(!sl) return false;
  var $sl=$(sl);
  var _list=sl.querySelector('.slick-list');var _track=sl.querySelector('.slick-track');
  function syncBanner(idx){
    var realSlides=sl.querySelectorAll('.slick-slide:not(.slick-cloned)');
    var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
    if(idx<0||idx>=ctrlLis.length)return;
    realSlides.forEach(function(s,i){s.classList.toggle('banner-show',i===idx&&!s.classList.contains('slider-controller'));});
    ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){var a=(i===idx);li.className=a?'current':'';li.style.setProperty('background',a?'#F3C969':'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)','important');li.style.setProperty('width',a?'14px':'10px','important');li.style.setProperty('height',a?'14px':'10px','important');});
  }
  $sl.off('afterChange.ksbnav').on('afterChange.ksbnav',function(e,slick,cur){setTimeout(function(){syncBanner(cur);},50);});
  var prevBtn=sl.querySelector('.slick-prev');
  var nextBtn=sl.querySelector('.slick-next');
  if(prevBtn){prevBtn.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');prevBtn.style.setProperty('z-index','20','important');prevBtn.addEventListener('click',function(){$sl.slick('slickPrev');});}
  if(nextBtn){nextBtn.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');nextBtn.style.setProperty('z-index','20','important');nextBtn.addEventListener('click',function(){$sl.slick('slickNext');});}
  var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
  ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){
    li.style.cursor='pointer';
    li.onclick=function(){$sl.slick('slickGoTo',i);};
  });
  syncBanner(0);
  return true;
}
var _bnTries=0;
function tryBannerNav(){if(!initBannerNav()&&_bnTries++<30) setTimeout(tryBannerNav,300);}
tryBannerNav();
})();

(function(){
var _answers = {};

function scoreProduct(p, a){
  var score = 0;
  if(p.category.indexOf(a.needType||'mattress')>=0) score+=100;
  if(p.sizes&&p.sizes[a.size||'casal']) score+=50;
  if(p.comfort.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=30;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.painPoint||'comfort')>=0) score+=40;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=20;
  if(p.bestFor.indexOf(a.sleepPos||'side')>=0) score+=25;
  if((a.painPoint==='delivery')&&p.format==='box_mattress') score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='partner_motion')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='heat'||a.comfort==='cooling')&&p.coolingTechnology) score+=50;
  if((a.sleepPos==='couple'||a.sleepPos==='moves')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=30;
  return score;
}
function formatMoney(v){ return 'R$ '+v; }
function offPct(de,por){
  var d=parseFloat(de.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  var p=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(d)||isNaN(p)||d<=0) return '';
  return Math.round((1-p/d)*100)+'% OFF';
}
function nightCost(por,years){
  if(!years||years<0.5) return '';
  var num=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(num)) return '';
  var nights=years*365;
  var n=(num/nights).toFixed(2).replace('.',',');
  return '&#127769; R$ '+n+'/noite por '+years+(years===1?' ano':' anos');
}
function chips(p){
  var arr=[];
  if(p.trialNights>0) arr.push('&#127769; '+p.trialNights+' noites de teste');
  if(p.guaranteeYears>=1) arr.push('&#128737; '+p.guaranteeYears+(p.guaranteeYears<2?' ano':' anos')+' de garantia');
  else if(p.guaranteeYears>0) arr.push('&#128737; 3 meses de garantia');
  arr.push('&#128666; Frete gr&aacute;tis');
  if(p.format==='box_mattress') arr.push('&#128230; Cabe em elevador');
  return arr;
}

function profileChips(){
  var a=_answers;
  var arr=[];
  var isPillow=!!(a.pSleep||a.pPain||a.pFeel);
  if(isPillow){
    var sm={side:'Dorme de lado',back:'Dorme de costas',stomach:'Dorme de barriga',mixed:'Muda de posição'};
    var pm={neck_pain:'Dor no pescoço',heat:'Sente calor',sinks:'Travesseiro afunda',softness:'Prefere maciez',support:'Precisa de suporte',posture:'Ronca ou postura',cuddling:'Dorme abraçado'};
    var fm={very_soft:'Muito macio',balanced:'Equilibrado',firm:'Mais firme',cooling:'Fresco e ventilado'};
    if(a.pSleep&&sm[a.pSleep]) arr.push(sm[a.pSleep]);
    if(a.pPain&&pm[a.pPain]) arr.push(pm[a.pPain]);
    if(a.pFeel&&fm[a.pFeel]) arr.push(fm[a.pFeel]);
  } else {
    var smap={side:'Dorme de lado',back:'Dorme de costas',stomach:'Dorme de bruços',couple:'Casal',moves:'Se mexe muito'};
    var cmap={soft:'Prefere macio',firm:'Prefere firme',medium:'Preferê intermediário',cooling:'Sofre de calor'};
    var pmap={back_pain:'Tem dores nas costas',heat:'Sofre de calor',partner_motion:'Parceiro se mexe',sagging:'Colchão afundou',delivery:'Precisa de entrega fácil',comfort:'Busca mais conforto'};
    if(a.sleepPos&&smap[a.sleepPos]) arr.push(smap[a.sleepPos]);
    if(a.comfort&&cmap[a.comfort]) arr.push(cmap[a.comfort]);
    if(a.painPoint&&pmap[a.painPoint]) arr.push(pmap[a.painPoint]);
    if(a.size) arr.push('Tamanho: '+a.size.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+a.size.slice(1));
  }
  return arr;
}function showStep(step){
  var all=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-q');
  for(var i=0;i<all.length;i++) all[i].classList.remove('active');
  if(step==='results'){
    showResults();
  } else {
    var el=document.getElementById('ksq-'+step);
    if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  }
}

  if(!matching.length) matching=scored;

  if(matching[0]) slots.push({item:matching[0].p,badge:'best',label:'MAIS RECOMENDADO',score:matching[0].score});

    {id:'toque_eleva',name:'Toque Eleva',type:'pillow',height:'medium',
     bestFor:['back','mixed','stomach'],cooling:false,supportLevel:'low',softFeel:true,
     strengths:['softness','fluffy','comfort'],therapeutic:false,

     url:'/travesseiro-toque-eleva',
     price:7900,priceFrom:15800,
     benefits:['Ultra macio','Confortavel','Acessivel','Facil lavar']}
  ];
  catalog.forEach(function(p){
    var sc=0;
    if(sleep&&p.bestFor.indexOf(sleep)>=0) sc+=40;
    if(pain==='neck_pain'&&p.strengths.indexOf('cervical')>=0) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='neck_pain'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=25;
    if(pain==='heat'&&p.cooling) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='heat'&&p.strengths.indexOf('cooling')>=0) sc+=15;
    if(pain==='sinks'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=40;
    if(pain==='sinks'&&p.supportLevel==='medium') sc+=20;
    if(pain==='softness'&&p.softFeel) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='support'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=50;
    if(pain==='posture'&&p.therapeutic) sc+=40;
    if(pain==='posture'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=20;
    if(pain==='cuddling'&&p.softFeel) sc+=30;
    if(pain==='cuddling'&&(p.id==='double_latex'||p.id==='pluma_sense')) sc+=20;
    if(feel==='very_soft'&&p.softFeel) sc+=30;
    if(feel==='balanced'&&p.supportLevel==='medium') sc+=30;
    if(feel==='firm'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=30;
    if(feel==='cooling'&&p.cooling) sc+=40;
    if(pref==='tech'&&(p.id==='memory_foam'||p.id==='purple_pillow')) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='fluffy'&&p.softFeel) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='natural'&&(p.id==='latex_flakes'||p.id==='double_latex')) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='value'&&p.premiumLevel==='essencial') sc+=25;
    if(budget==='essential'&&p.premiumLevel==='essencial') sc+=20;
    if(budget==='premium'&&p.premiumLevel==='premium') sc+=20;
    if(budget==='max'&&p.premiumLevel==='premium'&&p.price>=22900) sc+=20;
    if(sleep==='stomach'){
      if(p.height==='low') sc+=30;
      if(p.softFeel) sc+=15;
    }
    if(sleep==='side'){
      if(p.height==='medium'||p.height==='high') sc+=15;
      if(p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=15;
    }
    if(sleep==='back'){
      if(p.height==='medium') sc+=20;
    }
    p.score=sc;
  });
  catalog.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});
  return catalog;
}function handleClick(e){
  var opt=e.target.closest('.ksq-opt');
  if(!opt) return;
  var key=opt.getAttribute('data-key');
  var val=opt.getAttribute('data-val');
  var next=opt.getAttribute('data-next');
  if(key&&val) _answers[key]=val;
  var sibs=opt.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<sibs.length;i++) sibs[i].classList.remove('selected');
  opt.classList.add('selected');
  if(next==='results'){
    setTimeout(showResults,200);
  } else if(next){
    setTimeout(function(){showStep(next);},200);
  }
}

function handleRestart(){
  _answers={};
  var opts=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<opts.length;i++) opts[i].classList.remove('selected');
  var rEl=document.getElementById('ksq-results-area');
  if(rEl) rEl.classList.remove('active');
  showStep('q0');
  setTimeout(function(){var ksqW=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');if(ksqW){var top=ksqW.getBoundingClientRect().top+document.documentElement.scrollTop-20;document.documentElement.scrollTop=top;}},50);
}

function init(){
  var wrap=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');
  if(!wrap){setTimeout(init,300);return;}
  wrap.addEventListener('click',handleClick);
  var rb=document.getElementById('ksq-restart-btn');
  if(rb) rb.addEventListener('click',handleRestart);
  showStep('q0');
}

function tryInit(){if(document.getElementById('ksq-wrap')){init();}else{setTimeout(tryInit,100);}}
if(document.readyState==='loading'){
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,200);});
} else {
  setTimeout(tryInit,200);
}
window.addEventListener('load',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,500);});
})();
</script>

(function(){
function initBannerNav(){
  var sl=document.querySelector('.full-banner.newBanners .wd-marketing-banner.slider.slick-initialized');
  if(!sl) return false;
  var $sl=$(sl);
  function syncBanner(idx){
    var realSlides=sl.querySelectorAll('.slick-slide:not(.slick-cloned)');
    var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
    if(idx<0||idx>=ctrlLis.length)return;
    realSlides.forEach(function(s,i){s.classList.toggle('banner-show',i===idx&&!s.classList.contains('slider-controller'));});
    ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){var a=(i===idx);li.className=a?'current':'';li.style.setProperty('background',a?'#F3C969':'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)','important');li.style.setProperty('width',a?'14px':'10px','important');li.style.setProperty('height',a?'14px':'10px','important');});
  }
  $sl.off('afterChange.ksbnav').on('afterChange.ksbnav',function(e,slick,cur){setTimeout(function(){syncBanner(cur);},50);});
  var ctrlLis=sl.querySelectorAll('.slider-controller:not(.slick-cloned) li');
  ctrlLis.forEach(function(li,i){
    li.style.cursor='pointer';
    li.onclick=function(){$sl.slick('slickGoTo',i);};
  });
  syncBanner(0);
  return true;
}
var _bnTries=0;
function tryBannerNav(){if(!initBannerNav()&&_bnTries++<30) setTimeout(tryBannerNav,300);}
tryBannerNav();
})();

(function(){
var _answers = {};

function scoreProduct(p, a){
  var score = 0;
  if(p.category.indexOf(a.needType||'mattress')>=0) score+=100;
  if(p.sizes&&p.sizes[a.size||'casal']) score+=50;
  if(p.comfort.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=30;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.painPoint||'comfort')>=0) score+=40;
  if(p.strengths.indexOf(a.comfort||'medium')>=0) score+=20;
  if(p.bestFor.indexOf(a.sleepPos||'side')>=0) score+=25;
  if((a.painPoint==='delivery')&&p.format==='box_mattress') score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='partner_motion')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=50;
  if((a.painPoint==='heat'||a.comfort==='cooling')&&p.coolingTechnology) score+=50;
  if((a.sleepPos==='couple'||a.sleepPos==='moves')&&p.hasPocketSprings) score+=30;
  return score;
}
function formatMoney(v){ return 'R$ '+v; }
function offPct(de,por){
  var d=parseFloat(de.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  var p=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(d)||isNaN(p)||d<=0) return '';
  return Math.round((1-p/d)*100)+'% OFF';
}
function nightCost(por,years){
  if(!years||years<0.5) return '';
  var num=parseFloat(por.replace(/\./g,'').replace(',','.'));
  if(isNaN(num)) return '';
  var nights=years*365;
  var n=(num/nights).toFixed(2).replace('.',',');
  return '&#127769; R$ '+n+'/noite por '+years+(years===1?' ano':' anos');
}
function chips(p){
  var arr=[];
  if(p.trialNights>0) arr.push('&#127769; '+p.trialNights+' noites de teste');
  if(p.guaranteeYears>=1) arr.push('&#128737; '+p.guaranteeYears+(p.guaranteeYears<2?' ano':' anos')+' de garantia');
  else if(p.guaranteeYears>0) arr.push('&#128737; 3 meses de garantia');
  arr.push('&#128666; Frete gr&aacute;tis');
  if(p.format==='box_mattress') arr.push('&#128230; Cabe em elevador');
  return arr;

function profileChips(){
  var a=_answers;
  var arr=[];
  var isPillow=!!(a.pSleep||a.pPain||a.pFeel);
  if(isPillow){
    var sm={side:'Dorme de lado',back:'Dorme de costas',stomach:'Dorme de barriga',mixed:'Muda de posição'};
    var pm={neck_pain:'Dor no pescoço',heat:'Sente calor',sinks:'Travesseiro afunda',softness:'Prefere maciez',support:'Precisa de suporte',posture:'Ronca ou postura',cuddling:'Dorme abraçado'};
    var fm={very_soft:'Muito macio',balanced:'Equilibrado',firm:'Mais firme',cooling:'Fresco e ventilado'};
    if(a.pSleep&&sm[a.pSleep]) arr.push(sm[a.pSleep]);
    if(a.pPain&&pm[a.pPain]) arr.push(pm[a.pPain]);
    if(a.pFeel&&fm[a.pFeel]) arr.push(fm[a.pFeel]);
  } else {
    var smap={side:'Dorme de lado',back:'Dorme de costas',stomach:'Dorme de bruços',couple:'Casal',moves:'Se mexe muito'};
    var cmap={soft:'Prefere macio',firm:'Prefere firme',medium:'Preferê intermediário',cooling:'Sofre de calor'};
    var pmap={back_pain:'Tem dores nas costas',heat:'Sofre de calor',partner_motion:'Parceiro se mexe',sagging:'Colchão afundou',delivery:'Precisa de entrega fácil',comfort:'Busca mais conforto'};
    if(a.sleepPos&&smap[a.sleepPos]) arr.push(smap[a.sleepPos]);
    if(a.comfort&&cmap[a.comfort]) arr.push(cmap[a.comfort]);
    if(a.painPoint&&pmap[a.painPoint]) arr.push(pmap[a.painPoint]);
    if(a.size) arr.push('Tamanho: '+a.size.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+a.size.slice(1));
  }
  return arr;
}function showStep(step){
  var all=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-q');
  for(var i=0;i<all.length;i++) all[i].classList.remove('active');
  if(step==='results'){
    showResults();
  } else {
    var el=document.getElementById('ksq-'+step);
    if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  }
}

  var scored = catalog.map(function(p){ return {p:p,score:scoreProduct(p,a)}; });
  scored.sort(function(x,y){ return y.score-x.score; });
  var matching = scored.filter(function(x){ return x.p.category.indexOf(a.needType||'mattress')>=0; });
  if(!matching.length) matching=scored;
  
  var slots=[];
  if(matching[0]) slots.push({item:matching[0].p,badge:'best',label:'MAIS RECOMENDADO',score:matching[0].score});
  var notS1=matching.filter(function(x){return !slots.some(function(s){return s.item===x.p;});});
  var budget=notS1.filter(function(x){return x.p.tier==='entry'||x.p.tier==='mid';});
  var s2=budget[0]||notS1[0];
  if(s2) slots.push({item:s2.p,badge:'good',label:'MELHOR CUSTO-BENEF&Iacute;CIO',score:s2.score});
  var notSel=matching.filter(function(x){return !slots.some(function(s){return s.item===x.p;});});

  chips.forEach(function(c){html+='<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;background:rgba(255,215,100,.08);border:1px solid rgba(255,215,100,.2);border-radius:20px;padding:3px 10px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.75);font-size:.8em"><span style="color:#4ade80">\u2714</span>'+c+'</span>';});
  html+='</div></div></div>';
  profBlock.innerHTML=html;
  var discEl=document.getElementById('ksq-discarded');
  if(discEl){
    var discMap={
      firm:'modelos muito macios, que n\u00e3o oferecem suporte suficiente para sua coluna',
      soft:'modelos firmes, que poderiam gerar press\u00e3o nos pontos de contato',
      medium:'modelos nos extremos de firmeza, focando no equil\u00edbrio',
      cooling:'modelos sem ventila\u00e7\u00e3o, que reteriam calor',
      back_pain:'modelos sem molas ensacadas, com menor capacidade de suporte postural',
      heat:'modelos com espuma tradicionais, que ret\u00eam mais calor',
      partner_motion:'modelos de espuma s\u00f3lida, que transferem mais movimento',
      sagging:'modelos de menor densidade, que afundariam mais rapidamente',
      stomach:'modelos muito macios, que causariam hiperextens\u00e3o na coluna lombar'
    };
    var reason=discMap[a.painPoint]||discMap[a.comfort]||discMap[a.sleepPos];
    if(reason){
      discEl.innerHTML='<div style="background:rgba(255,100,100,.05);border:1px solid rgba(255,100,100,.15);border-radius:10px;padding:14px 16px;margin:16px auto;max-width:1100px">'+'<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.45);font-size:.75em;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">Por que os outros foram descartados</div>'+'<div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.65);font-size:.85em;line-height:1.5">N\u00e3o recomendamos '+reason+', com base no seu perfil informado.</div></div>';
    }
  }
})();
  var rEl=document.getElementById('ksq-results-area');
  if(rEl) rEl.classList.add('active');
}

  catalog.forEach(function(p){
    var sc=0;
    if(sleep&&p.bestFor.indexOf(sleep)>=0) sc+=40;
    if(pain==='neck_pain'&&p.strengths.indexOf('cervical')>=0) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='neck_pain'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=25;
    if(pain==='heat'&&p.cooling) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='heat'&&p.strengths.indexOf('cooling')>=0) sc+=15;
    if(pain==='sinks'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=40;
    if(pain==='sinks'&&p.supportLevel==='medium') sc+=20;
    if(pain==='softness'&&p.softFeel) sc+=50;
    if(pain==='support'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=50;
    if(pain==='posture'&&p.therapeutic) sc+=40;
    if(pain==='posture'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=20;
    if(pain==='cuddling'&&p.softFeel) sc+=30;
    if(pain==='cuddling'&&(p.id==='double_latex'||p.id==='pluma_sense')) sc+=20;
    if(feel==='very_soft'&&p.softFeel) sc+=30;
    if(feel==='balanced'&&p.supportLevel==='medium') sc+=30;
    if(feel==='firm'&&p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=30;
    if(feel==='cooling'&&p.cooling) sc+=40;
    if(pref==='tech'&&(p.id==='memory_foam'||p.id==='purple_pillow')) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='fluffy'&&p.softFeel) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='natural'&&(p.id==='latex_flakes'||p.id==='double_latex')) sc+=25;
    if(pref==='value'&&p.premiumLevel==='essencial') sc+=25;
    if(budget==='essential'&&p.premiumLevel==='essencial') sc+=20;
    if(budget==='premium'&&p.premiumLevel==='premium') sc+=20;
    if(budget==='max'&&p.premiumLevel==='premium'&&p.price>=22900) sc+=20;
    if(sleep==='stomach'){
      if(p.height==='low') sc+=30;
      if(p.softFeel) sc+=15;
    }
    if(sleep==='side'){
      if(p.height==='medium'||p.height==='high') sc+=15;
      if(p.supportLevel==='high') sc+=15;
    }
    if(sleep==='back'){
      if(p.height==='medium') sc+=20;
    }
    p.score=sc;
  });
  catalog.sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;});
  return catalog;
}function handleClick(e){
  var opt=e.target.closest('.ksq-opt');
  if(!opt) return;
  var key=opt.getAttribute('data-key');
  var val=opt.getAttribute('data-val');
  var next=opt.getAttribute('data-next');
  if(key&&val) _answers[key]=val;
  var sibs=opt.parentElement.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<sibs.length;i++) sibs[i].classList.remove('selected');
  opt.classList.add('selected');
  if(next==='results'){
    setTimeout(showResults,200);
  } else if(next){
    setTimeout(function(){showStep(next);},200);
  }
}

function handleRestart(){
  _answers={};
  var opts=document.querySelectorAll('.ksq-opt');
  for(var i=0;i<opts.length;i++) opts[i].classList.remove('selected');
  var rEl=document.getElementById('ksq-results-area');
  if(rEl) rEl.classList.remove('active');
  showStep('q0');
  setTimeout(function(){var ksqW=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');if(ksqW){var top=ksqW.getBoundingClientRect().top+document.documentElement.scrollTop-20;document.documentElement.scrollTop=top;}},50);
}

function init(){
  var wrap=document.getElementById('ksq-wrap');
  if(!wrap){setTimeout(init,300);return;}
  wrap.addEventListener('click',handleClick);
  var rb=document.getElementById('ksq-restart-btn');
  if(rb) rb.addEventListener('click',handleRestart);
  showStep('q0');
}

function tryInit(){if(document.getElementById('ksq-wrap')){init();}else{setTimeout(tryInit,100);}}
if(document.readyState==='loading'){
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,200);});
} else {
  setTimeout(tryInit,200);
}
window.addEventListener('load',function(){setTimeout(tryInit,500);});
})();
</script>
